227075 1110-FETCH-ITIN-PORT.
227075  
227075     MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SUB.   
227075  
227075     EXEC SQL WHENEVER SQLERROR CONTINUE END-EXEC.

227075     EXEC SQL DECLARE ITIN_PORT CURSOR
227075          SELECT IFNULL(PT.REQ_VISA_CODE,' ') 
227075          FROM   ITINERARY_DETAILS ITN
227075          JOIN   PORT PT  
227075          ON     PT.PORT_CODE = ITN.PORT_CODE 
227075          WHERE  ITN.EMBARK_PORT_CODE = :TDP01-EMBARK-PORT-CODE   
227075             AND ITN.DURATION_DAYS    = :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS  
227075             AND ITN.ITINERARY_CODE   = :TDP01-ITINERARY-CODE 
227075     END-EXEC.
227075  
227075     EXEC SQL 
227075          OPEN ITIN_PORT  
227075     END-EXEC.
227075  
227075     IF SQLCODE < 0   
227075        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
227075     END-IF.  
227075  
227075 1120-NEXT-ITIN-PORT. 
227075  
227075     EXEC SQL 
227075          FETCH NEXT  
227075          FROM  ITIN_PORT 
227075          INTO :WS-REQ-VISA-CATEGORY  
227075     END-EXEC.

227075     IF SQLCODE < 0   
227075        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
227075     ELSE 
227075        IF SQLCODE = 100  
227075           GO TO 1130-CLOSE-CURSOR
227075        END-IF
227075     END-IF.  

227075     IF WS-REQ-VISA-CATEGORY NOT > SPACES 
227075        GO TO 1120-NEXT-ITIN-PORT 
227075     END-IF.  
227075  
227075     ADD 1 TO WS-SUB. 
227075  
227075     IF WS-SUB > WS-REQ-VISA-COUNT-PORT-MAX   
227075        GO TO 1130-CLOSE-CURSOR   
227075     ELSE 
227075        MOVE WS-REQ-VISA-CATEGORY TO WS-REQ-VISA-CAT-PORT (WS-SUB)
227075     END-IF.  
227075  
227075     MOVE WS-SUB TO WS-REQ-VISA-CAT-COUNT-PORT.   
227075  
227075     GO TO 1120-NEXT-ITIN-PORT.   
227075  
227075 1130-CLOSE-CURSOR.   
227075  
227075     EXEC SQL CLOSE ITIN_PORT END-EXEC.   
227075  
227075     IF SQLCODE < 0   
227075        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
227075     END-IF.  
227075  
227075     EXEC SQL WHENEVER SQLERROR   
227075       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
227075     END-EXEC.

                    (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO RSP06-PIID-EXP-DATE   
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-DATE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-DATE-SUB > 6
               MOVE ZEROES             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE   
               MOVE RSP06-PICF-DEPART-DATE OF RSP06-OLD 
                    (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO RSP06-PICF-DEPART-DATE
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
               MOVE ZEROES             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE   
               MOVE RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE OF RSP06-OLD 
                    (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-DATE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-DATE-SUB > 2
               MOVE ZEROES             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE   
               MOVE RSP06-PICH-CHECK-IN-DATE
                    OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)   
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO RSP06-PICH-CHECK-IN-DATE  
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
               MOVE ZEROES             TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE   
               MOVE RSP06-PICH-CHECK-OUT-DATE   
                    OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)   
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO RSP06-PICH-CHECK-OUT-DATE 
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE ZEROES                 TO DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                                       TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.  
           PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY.  
           MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE    TO RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE  
                                          OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB).

       1400-DATE-IN-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP.  
      D    MOVE '1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP'   TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

           ADD   1                     TO  ABSPL1-SUB.  

           IF  ABSPL1-SUB > ABSPL1-OCCURS   OR  
               ABSPL1-SUB > ABSPL1-SEGMENT-MAX  
               GO TO 1500-PROCESS-SPC-SERV-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

           IF  ABSPL1-SEGMENT (ABSPL1-SUB) NOT > SPACES 
               GO TO 1500-PROCESS-SPC-SERV-EXIT 
           END-IF.  

           IF  ABSPL1-PAX-SEQ-NUM (ABSPL1-SUB) NOT =
                         RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
               GO TO 1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP 
           END-IF.

           MOVE ABSPL1-SERVICE-CODE (ABSPL1-SUB)
                                       TO WS-SPC-SVC-CODE.

                   CCP11-SERVICE-EXIT.  

           MOVE ABSPL1-SERVICE-CODE (ABSPL1-SUB)
                                       TO WS-SP-OTH-CODE.   

           IF  ( WS-SP-OTH-CODE (1:2) = 'XF' AND
                 WS-SP-OTH-CODE (6:1) = 'I'  OR 'O' )  OR   
C92560           WS-OTH-CODE  OR
C92560         ( WS-SP-OTH-CODE (1:2) = 'FP')   
               GO TO 1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZERO                   TO WS-TALLY. 
           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
               TALLYING WS-TALLY FOR ALL WS-COMMENT-CODE.   

           IF  WS-TALLY > ZERO  
               GO TO 1520-CONT-SPC-SERV 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-VCR-CODE-FND.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-VCR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-VCR-SUB > WS-VCR-CTR OR WS-VCR-CODE-FND = 'Y'   
               IF  WS-COMMENT-CODE =
                                   WS-VCR-MISC-COMMENT-CODE (WS-VCR-SUB)
                   MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-VCR-CODE-FND   
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-VCR-CODE-FND = 'N'
               GO TO 1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP 
           END-IF.

           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
               REPLACING FIRST SPACE BY WS-COMMENT-CODE.

C10221     IF WS-COMMENT-CODE = 'Q' OR 'X'  
C10221        MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-PREGNANCY-IND.  

           GO TO 1510-SPC-SERV-LOOP.

C89405  
C89405*    NOTE:  The following IF statement is required due to present 
C89405*    limitations in FunPass front end.
C89405* 
C89405*    The FunPass front end can cause this program to be invoked   
C89405*    via either the Booking Engine or via a Web Service.However   
C89405*    the Booking Engine has not been modified to send the new 
C89405*    DOC name fields.Only when FunPass is building a Web  
C89405*    Service request will it provide the DOC names fields in the  
C89405*    XML message. 
C89405* 
C89405*    However, this program doesn't have a clear way of knowing
C89405*    how it was invoked.We will just assume that, if the  
C89405*    DOC-FIRST and LAST names are both blank, then it must have   
C89405*    been via Booking Engine and no update to those fields are
C89405*    intended.
C89405* 
C89405*    WHEN, a few months in the future from Feb.2010, FunPass  
C89405*    is reworked to always use the Web Service.Then it should 
C89405*    consistently send the DOC names.THEN, the IF condition can   
C89405*    can be removed and ELSE block discarded. 
C89405

      *THIS PARA VALIDATES THE NATIONALITY OF THE PAX.                 *
      ******************************************************************

       2600-PROCESS-NATIONALITY.
      D    MOVE '2600-PROCESS-NATIONALITY'  
      D                                TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 2600-PROCESS-NATIONALITY ' UPON PRINTER. 

           MOVE SPACES                 TO AAR-CUSTOMER-KEY. 
           MOVE 'CM'                   TO AAR-CUSTOMER-TYPE.

           IF  WS-BOOK-TYPE = 'I'   
               MOVE IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM   TO AAR-CUSTOMER-NUM  
           ELSE 
               MOVE GBR1-CUSTOMER-NUM  TO AAR-CUSTOMER-NUM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 12111008               TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER.   

           FETCH AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD  
               ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.  

           MOVE 10012109               TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER.   

           FETCH OWNER WITHIN AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-SET SET
               ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.  

           MOVE SPACE                  TO WS-PP-GRATS-IND   
                                          WS-PP-GRATS-IND-TOO.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'US'  
               MOVE RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD 
               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)        TO NR-NATIONALITY-CODE   
               MOVE 43501010           TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER

               FETCH NATIONALITY-REC RECORD 
                   ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR   

               IF  AGENT-INTL-SALES-IND = 'Y' OR
                   AGENT-REGION-CODE = 6
                   IF  NR-PP-GRATS-REQ-IND = 'Y'
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-PP-GRATS-IND   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  GSTINF-NATIONALITY-CODE (WS-SUB1) NOT = 'US' 
               MOVE GSTINF-NATIONALITY-CODE (WS-SUB1)   
                                       TO NR-NATIONALITY-CODE   
               MOVE 43501011           TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER

               FETCH NATIONALITY-REC RECORD 
                   ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR   

               IF  AGENT-INTL-SALES-IND = 'Y' OR
                   AGENT-REGION-CODE = 6
                   IF  NR-PP-GRATS-REQ-IND = 'Y'
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-PP-GRATS-IND-TOO   
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-PP-GRATS-IND = WS-PP-GRATS-IND-TOO
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE 11                 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
               MOVE 'Y'                TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
           END-IF.  

       2600-PROCESS-NATIONALITY-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

                       END-IF   
                       IF  RSP06-PAX-AGE
                           OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  NOT   > 0 
                           IF  DPP02-YEAR-CNT < 2   
                               MOVE DPP02-MONTH-CNT 
                                       TO RSP06-PAX-AGE 
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                          ELSE

                                       TO RSP06-PAX-AGE 
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                          END-IF
                       END-IF   
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-AGE  OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                       TO GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1)   
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                       TO GSTINF-AGE-CODE (WS-SUB1) 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE
                                           OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                       TO GSTINF-BIRTH-DATE (WS-SUB1)   
                       MOVE CCP01-DATE TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
                       MOVE 14         TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS 
                       PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
                       IF  ( IBR-BOOK-DATE  >= 20080101) OR 
                           ( GBR1-BOOK-DATE >= 20080101)
PPC470                     IF (( RSP06-PAX-TITLE
PPC470                        OF RSP06-NEW(WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'INFT') AND
PPC470                         ( RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-NEW 
PPC470                         (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) < TDP01-MIN-INFANT-AGE))
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO WS-SHOW-MSG-SW
                           END-IF   
                           IF  SHOW-MSG 
                               IF  WS-SAIL-DATE > DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
                                   ADD 1
                                       TO CCMV-ADV-SUB

                                       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)

                                   MOVE 'NFX'

                                          GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE

                                       TO DPP04-HIST-COMMENT
                                          DPP04-TRF-COMMENT2
                                          GRP15-TRF-COMMENT1
                                          GRP15-COMMENT 
PPC470                             MOVE 'MONTHS'
PPC470                                 TO CCP00-AGE-CODE
PPC470                             MOVE TDP01-MIN-INFANT-AGE
PPC470                                 TO CCP00-AGE-MIN 
                                   MOVE WS-SUB1 
                                       TO CCP00-PAX-NUM 
                                   ADD 1
                                       TO CCMV-ADV-SUB

                                       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                                   PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG THRU   
                                           CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT

                               END-IF   
                           END-IF   
                       ELSE 
                           IF  RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-OLD   
                               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  < 4                 AND
                               RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
                               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'M'   
                               IF  WS-SAIL-DATE > DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
                                   ADD 1
                                       TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
                                   MOVE 0065
                                       TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE
                                                          (CCMV-ADV-SUB)
                                   MOVE 'MONTHS'
                                       TO CCP00-AGE-CODE
                                   MOVE 4

                                          GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE

                                          DPP04-TRF-COMMENT2

                                          GRP15-COMMENT

                                           2800-STORE-HISTORY-EXIT  
                               ELSE

                                       TO WS-WRITE-ERR-HIST 
                                   MOVE 'NFX'

                                          GRP15-ACTIVITY-CODE

                   END-IF

           ELSE 
               IF  GSTINF-BIRTH-DATE (WS-SUB1) NOT =
                   RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)

           IF  WS-BOOK-TYPE    = 'I'                                 AND

144449         MOVE SSP03-O-LOCATION-CODE  TO DPP04-TRF-LOCATION-CODE   
144449         MOVE SSP03-O-AGENCY-CONN-CODE
                                       TO DPP04-HIST-AGENCY-CONN-CODE

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA VALIDATES THE PAX AGE.                                *
      ******************************************************************

       2900-CHECK-AGE.  
      D    MOVE '2900-CHECK-AGE'       TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 2900-CHECK-AGE ' UPON PRINTER.   

           IF  GSTINF-AGE-CODE (WS-SUB1) = 'M'  
               IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 24 
                   MOVE 'I'            TO WS-AGE-IN 
               ELSE 
                       IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 156
                           MOVE 'C'    TO WS-AGE-IN 
                       ELSE 
                           IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 204     AND
                               RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-OLD 
                               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'S' OR 'G' 
                               MOVE 'S' 
                                       TO WS-AGE-IN 
                           ELSE 
                               MOVE 'A' 
                                       TO WS-AGE-IN 
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
PPC470*            END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  GSTINF-AGE-CODE (WS-SUB1) = 'Y'  
               IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 2  
                   MOVE 'I'            TO WS-AGE-IN 
               ELSE 
                       IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 13 
                           MOVE 'C'    TO WS-AGE-IN 
                       ELSE 
                           IF  GSTINF-AGE-AMOUNT (WS-SUB1) < 17      AND
                               RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-OLD 
                               (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'S' OR 'G' 
                               MOVE 'S' 
                                       TO WS-AGE-IN 
                           ELSE 
                               MOVE 'A' 
                                       TO WS-AGE-IN 
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-SEX OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'I'
               MOVE 'I' TO WS-AGE-OLD   
           ELSE 
                   IF  RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) < 13 
                       MOVE 'C'        TO WS-AGE-OLD
                   ELSE 
                       IF  RSP06-PAX-AGE OF RSP06-OLD   
                           (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) < 17                     AND
                           RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND OF RSP06-OLD 
                           (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'Y' OR 'S' OR 'G' 
                           MOVE 'S'    TO WS-AGE-OLD
                       ELSE 
                           MOVE 'A'    TO WS-AGE-OLD
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-AGE-IN NOT = WS-AGE-OLD   
               MOVE 13                 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
               MOVE 'Y'                TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
           END-IF.  

       2900-CHECK-AGE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

eNOAD                                  TO DPP03-PAX-AGE-CODE.   
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)

C93132     MOVE RSP06-PAX-TITLE          OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)

C93132     MOVE 'N'                    TO PSP01-OVERRIDE-NAME-CHECK

C93132                                    PSP01-NAME-MISMATCH-FLAG.

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA VALIDATES INPUT PHONE NUMBER OF THE PAX.              *
      ******************************************************************

       3300-VALIDATE-PHONE. 
      D    MOVE '3300-VALIDATE-PHONE'  TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 3300-VALIDATE-PHONE ' UPON PRINTER.  

148695     MOVE ZEROES                 TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM-N. 
148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-FROM-SUB FROM 20 BY -1
148695             UNTIL WS-FROM-SUB < 1
148695        MOVE WS-PPHOR-PHONE-NUMBER (WS-FROM-SUB:1)
148695                                 TO WS-TEMP-NUM-DIGIT 
148695        IF  WS-TEMP-NUM-DIGIT NOT < ZERO AND  
148695            WS-TEMP-NUM-DIGIT NOT > 9 
148695          MOVE WS-TEMP-NUM-DIGIT TO   
148695                               WS-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM-N (WS-TO-SUB:1) 
148695          SUBTRACT 1            FROM WS-TO-SUB
148695        END-IF
148695     END-PERFORM.

148695                                                (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB 1)

148695                                                (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB 1)

148695     END-IF.

148695                                (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB) = 'H'

148695                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB)

148695                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB)

148695                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB)

              MOVE ZEROES              TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
              MOVE RSP06-PIID-ISSUE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW

              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE

                                       TO RSP06-PIID-EXP-DATE

               FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-DATE-SUB > 6
              MOVE ZEROES              TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
              MOVE RSP06-PICF-DEPART-DATE OF RSP06-NEW  
                   (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)

              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE TO RSP06-PICF-DEPART-DATE

              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE TO RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE

               MOVE ZEROES             TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               MOVE RSP06-PICH-CHECK-IN-DATE
                    OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB)

                                       TO RSP06-PICH-CHECK-OUT-DATE

           END-PERFORM.

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)

      *THIS PARA PROCESSES ALL THE MEMBERS OF THE OWNER OF THE CARBY   *
      *SEGMENT.                                                        *
      ******************************************************************

       4400-PROCESS-CACFOR-DATA.
      D    MOVE '4400-PROCESS-CACFOR-DATA'  
      D                                TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 4400-PROCESS-CACFOR-DATA ' UPON PRINTER. 

           IF  WS-FOUND-CACTBY AND WS-MOVED-CACFOR = 0  
               SUBTRACT 1              FROM CACTBY-SUB  
               PERFORM 4500-VERIFY-CACFOR THRU  
                       4500-VERIFY-CACFOR-EXIT  
               VARYING WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                   UNTIL WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB >
                                              CCP01-CAR-ACCEPT-FOR-COUNT
               IF  CACFOR-OCCURS > 1
                   PERFORM 4600-VERIFY-CFOR THRU
                           4600-VERIFY-CFOR-EXIT
                       VARYING WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                           UNTIL WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB > CACFOR-OCCURS  
               END-IF   
               MOVE 1                  TO WS-MOVED-CACFOR   
               MOVE ZEROES             TO CCP01-FOR-SUB 
               PERFORM VARYING CACFOR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL CACFOR-SUB > CACFOR-OCCURS 
                   IF  CACFOR-ACTION-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) = 'A' 
                       MOVE 0          TO CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT  
                                          CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM 
                       MOVE SPACES     TO CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM 
                       MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB)
                                       TO CSSXR-PAX-BKG-NUM 
                       MOVE CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO CSSXR-PAX-SEQ-NUM 
                       PERFORM 4700-FETCH-CSSXR THRU
                               4700-FETCH-CSSXR-EXIT
                       IF  WS-CSSXR-FOUND   
                           IF  CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT NOT  = 0          OR
                               CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM     > SPACES     OR
                               CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM NOT =  ZERO
                               MOVE 49 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
                               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                       IF  (( CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) =
                              RSP06-PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM  
                              OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)          AND
                              RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE   
                              OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'G' ))  OR
                           ( CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) = 
                             RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM 
                             OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)           AND
                             RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE
                             OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'G')   
                           MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR    (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-BOOK-NUM 
                           MOVE CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-SEQ-NUM  
                           MOVE CACFOR-ACTION-FOR      (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-ACTION   
                           MOVE 'CACFOR'
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-FLAG 
                           PERFORM 5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE THRU 
                                   5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE-EXIT 
                       ELSE 
                           MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB)
                                       TO WS-PAX-ON-SAIL-BKG-NUM
                           PERFORM 4800-CHECK-PAX-ON-SAIL THRU  
                                   4800-CHECK-PAX-ON-SAIL-EXIT  
                           IF  PAX-NOT-ON-SAILING   
                               MOVE 32 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
                               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
                           END-IF   
                           MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR    (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-BOOK-NUM 
                           MOVE CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-SEQ-NUM  
                           MOVE CACFOR-ACTION-FOR      (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-ACTION   
                           MOVE 'CACFOR'
                                       TO WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-FLAG 
                           PERFORM 5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE THRU 
                                   5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE-EXIT 
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
                   IF  CACFOR-ACTION-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) = 'A' OR  ' ' 
                       ADD 1           TO CCP01-FOR-SUB 
                       MOVE CACFOR-ACTION-FOR      (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-ACTION  
                                                   (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
                       MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR    (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-BKG-NUM 
                                                   (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
                       MOVE CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB) 
                                       TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-SEQ-NUM 
                                                   (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
220021                 MOVE CACFOR-FR-ACCEPT-IND   (CACFOR-SUB) 
220021                                 TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-FR-ACCEPT-IND   
220021                                             (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
174907                 IF CCP01-DATE = ZEROES   
174907                    MOVE WS-CCP01-DATE
174907                                 TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-DATE
174907                                              (CCP01-FOR-SUB) 
174907                 ELSE 
174907                    MOVE CCP01-DATE   
174907                                 TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-DATE
174907                                              (CCP01-FOR-SUB) 
174907                 END-IF   

174907                 IF CCP01-TIME-HHMMSS = ZEROES
174907                    MOVE WS-CCP01-TIME-HHMMSS 
174907                                 TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-TIME
174907                                         (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
174907                 ELSE 
174907                    MOVE CCP01-TIME-HHMMSS
174907                                 TO CCP01-CAR-FOR-TIME
174907                                         (CCP01-FOR-SUB)  
174907                 END-IF   


                       IF  CACTBY-PAX-SEQ-NUM (CACTBY-SUB) NOT =
                           RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   
                           (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)                          AND
                           ( CACTBY-FIRST-NAME-BY (CACTBY-SUB)  
                             NOT = SPACES                             OR
                             CACTBY-LAST-NAME-BY (CACTBY-SUB)   
                             NOT = SPACES ) 
                           MOVE 'Y'    TO CCP01-CSSXR-ACCEPT-AGENT-FLAG 
                                          WS-CSSXR-ACCEPT-AGENT-FLAG
                           MOVE CCP01-DATE  
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE   
                                          WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE  
                           MOVE CCP01-TIME-HHMMSS   
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME   
                                          WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME  
                           MOVE CACTBY-FIRST-NAME-BY (CACTBY-SUB)   
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME  
                                          WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME 
                           MOVE CACTBY-LAST-NAME-BY (CACTBY-SUB)
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME  
                                          WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME 
                       END-IF   
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE 55         TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
                       GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
               MOVE CCP01-FOR-SUB      TO CCP01-CAR-ACCEPT-FOR-COUNT
                                          WS-CAR-ACCEPT-FOR-COUNT   
           END-IF.  

       4400-PROCESS-CACFOR-DATA-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA VALIDATES IF ALL THE VALUE IN THE DATABASE EXIST IN   *
      *THE INPUT SEGMENT.                                              *
      ******************************************************************

       4500-VERIFY-CACFOR.  
      D    MOVE '4500-VERIFY-CACFOR'   TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 4500-VERIFY-CACFOR ' UPON PRINTER.   

           IF  CCP01-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                                           OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 4500-VERIFY-CACFOR-EXIT
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND-FLAG. 

           PERFORM VARYING CACFOR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL CACFOR-SUB > CACFOR-OCCURS OR CACFOR-SUB > 75 OR   
                   WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND  
               IF  CCP01-CAR-FOR-BKG-NUM (WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB) =  
                   CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB)                  AND
                   CCP01-CAR-FOR-SEQ-NUM (WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB) =  
                   CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB)  
                   MOVE 'YES'          TO WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND-FLAG  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-NOT-FOUND  
               MOVE 24                 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
               MOVE 'Y'                TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
            END-IF. 

       4500-VERIFY-CACFOR-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA VALIDATES IF THE SAME DATA IS NOT TWICE IN THE INPUT  *
      *SEGMENT.                                                        *
      ******************************************************************

       4600-VERIFY-CFOR.
      D    MOVE '4600-VERIFY-CFOR'     TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 4600-VERIFY-CFOR ' UPON PRINTER. 

           MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND-FLAG. 
           ADD 1                       TO WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB GIVING  
                                          WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB-1.  

           PERFORM VARYING CACFOR-SUB FROM WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB-1 BY 1 
               UNTIL CACFOR-SUB > CACFOR-OCCURS OR CACFOR-SUB > 75 OR   
                   WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND  
               IF  CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR    (WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB) = 
                   CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR    (CACFOR-SUB)               AND
                   CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB) = 
                   CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CACFOR-SUB)  
                   MOVE 'YES'          TO WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND-FLAG  
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-FOUND  
               MOVE 24                 TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
               MOVE 'Y'                TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
               GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
           END-IF.

PPC470     IF  IBR-SHIP-CODE     = TDP01-SHIP-CODE                   AND
PPC470         IBR-SAIL-DATE     = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD          AND
PPC470         IBR-DURATION-DAYS = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA LOOKS FOR THE ENTRY OF THE INCOMING BOOKINGS IN THE   *
      *TABLE AND IF THERE IS NO ENTRY FOR THE PARTICULAR ENTRY THEN    *
      *CREATES AN ENTRY IN TO THE TABLE FOR FUTURE PROCESSING.         *
      ******************************************************************

       5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE. 
      D    MOVE '5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE'  TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE ' UPON PRINTER.  

           MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-OBT-BOOK-PAX-SEQ-FIND-FLAG.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB > WS-OBT-BOOK-COUNT OR 
                   WS-OBT-BOOK-PAX-SEQ-FOUND
               IF  WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-BOOK-NUM =  
                   WS-OBT-BOOK-NUM (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-OBT-PAX-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                       UNTIL WS-OBT-PAX-SUB >   
                           WS-OBT-PAX-COUNT (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB) OR
                               WS-OBT-BOOK-PAX-SEQ-FOUND
                       IF  WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-SEQ-NUM  =  
                           WS-OBT-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                        (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB WS-OBT-PAX-SUB)
                           PERFORM 5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS THRU
                                   5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS-EXIT
                       END-IF   
                   END-PERFORM  
                   IF  WS-OBT-BOOK-PAX-SEQ-NOT-FOUND
                       ADD 1           TO   
                                      WS-OBT-PAX-COUNT (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)
                       MOVE WS-OBT-PAX-COUNT (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)  
                                       TO WS-OBT-PAX-SUB
                       MOVE WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-SEQ-NUM
                                       TO WS-OBT-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                                        (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB WS-OBT-PAX-SUB)
                       PERFORM 5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS THRU
                               5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS-EXIT
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-OBT-BOOK-PAX-SEQ-NOT-FOUND
               ADD 1                   TO WS-OBT-BOOK-COUNT 
               MOVE WS-OBT-BOOK-COUNT  TO WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB   
               MOVE WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-BOOK-NUM   
                                       TO   
                                       WS-OBT-BOOK-NUM (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)
               ADD 1                   TO   
                                      WS-OBT-PAX-COUNT (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)
               MOVE WS-OBT-PAX-COUNT (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB)  
                                       TO WS-OBT-PAX-SUB
               MOVE WS-OBT-CACFOR-ACTMEM-SEQ-NUM
                                       TO WS-OBT-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                                        (WS-OBT-BOOK-SUB WS-OBT-PAX-SUB)
               PERFORM 5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS THRU
                       5100-MOVE-OBT-FIELDS-EXIT
           END-IF.  

       5000-LOAD-OBT-TABLE-EXIT.
           EXIT.

                                   NOT > SPACES 
                                   CONTINUE 
                               ELSE 
                                   IF  CCP01-SSSR-SETUP-DATE > ZEROS
                                       MOVE 35  
                                       TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
                                       MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO CCP01-ERROR-IND

                               END-IF   
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF

               PERFORM 5500-VERIFY-SSSR THRU
                       5500-VERIFY-SSSR-EXIT
                   VARYING WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                       UNTIL WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB >
                                              CCP01-SSSR-SETUP-FOR-COUNT
               IF  ACTMEM-OCCURS > 1
                   PERFORM 5600-VERIFY-ACTMEM THRU  
                           5600-VERIFY-ACTMEM-EXIT  
                       VARYING WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                           UNTIL WS-CAR-SSSR-MATCH-SUB > ACTMEM-OCCURS  
               END-IF   
               PERFORM 5700-EDIT-ACTMEM-DATA THRU   
                       5700-EDIT-ACTMEM-DATA-EXIT   
                   VARYING ACTMEM-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                       UNTIL ACTMEM-SUB > ACTMEM-OCCURS 
               IF  CCP01-FOR-SUB > 0
                   MOVE CCP01-FOR-SUB  TO CCP01-SSSR-SETUP-FOR-COUNT
                                          WS-SSSR-SETUP-FOR-COUNT   
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 51             TO CCP01-MSG-NUM 
                   MOVE 'Y'            TO CCP01-ERROR-IND   
                   GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA LOADS THE CSSXR DETAILS IN RSP06 VARIABLES.           *
      ******************************************************************

       5800-LOAD-CSSXR-DETAILS. 
      D    MOVE '5800-LOAD-CSSXR-DETAILS'   
      D                                TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    DISPLAY 'AT 5800-LOAD-CSSXR-DETAILS ' UPON PRINTER.  

           MOVE SPACES                 TO CCP01-CSSXR-MOVE-TYPE-FLAG.   

           IF  CCP01-CAR-READ-IND = 'Y'                               OR
               WS-CAR-READ-IND = 'Y'
               PERFORM VARYING CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB > WS-CAR-ACCEPT-FOR-COUNT   
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-CSSXR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                       UNTIL WS-CSSXR-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                       IF  ( CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)   
                             = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW  
                                                                     AND
                             CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)
                             = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CSSXR-SUB)            )   
                           IF  ( CCP01-CAR-FOR-ACTION   
                                 (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 'A' OR 'C'  ) OR
                               ( CACFOR-ACTION-FOR  
                                 (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 'A' OR 'C'  )   
                               MOVE WS-CSSXR-SUB
                                       TO WS-OLD-PAX-SUB
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND 
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                               MOVE 'C' 
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-MOVE-TYPE-FLAG
                               PERFORM 5900-MOVE-CSSXR-FIELDS THRU  
                                       5900-MOVE-CSSXR-FIELDS-EXIT  
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                   END-PERFORM  
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           IF  CCP01-SSSR-READ-IND = 'Y'                              OR
               WS-SSSR-READ-IND = 'Y'   
               PERFORM VARYING CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB > WS-SSSR-SETUP-FOR-COUNT   
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-CSSXR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                       UNTIL WS-CSSXR-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
                       IF  ( ACTMEM-BOOK-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 
                             RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                                                     AND
                             ACTMEM-PAX-SEQ-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) =  
                             RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
                             (WS-CSSXR-SUB)                         )   
                           IF  ( CCP01-SSSR-SS-ACTION   
                                 (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = ' '           OR
                                 ACTMEM-ACTION (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) 
                                 = ' '                              )   
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND 
                                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CSSXR-SUB)
                           END-IF   
                           IF  ( CCP01-SSSR-SS-ACTION   
                                 (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 'A' OR 'C'    OR
                                 ACTMEM-ACTION (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) 
                                 = 'A' OR 'C'                       )   
                                                                     AND
                               ( CCP01-SSSR-ACTION NOT = 'D'        )   
                                                                     AND
                               ( WS-SSSR-ACTION NOT = 'D'           )   
                               MOVE 'Y' 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND 
                                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CSSXR-SUB)
                           ELSE 
                               IF  CCP01-SSSR-SS-ACTION 
                                   (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 'D'         OR
                                   CCP01-SSSR-ACTION = 'D'            OR
                                   ACTMEM-ACTION (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)   
                                   = 'D'                              OR
                                   WS-SSSR-ACTION = 'D' 
                                   MOVE 'N' 
                                      TO RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND  
                                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CSSXR-SUB)
                               END-IF   
                           END-IF   
                           IF  ( CCP01-SSSR-SS-ACTION   
                                 (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) = 'A' OR 'C'  
                                 OR 'D'                             ) OR
                               ( ACTMEM-ACTION (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) 
                                 = 'A' OR 'C'   
                                 OR 'D'                             )   
                               MOVE 'S' 
                                       TO CCP01-CSSXR-MOVE-TYPE-FLAG
                               IF  RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND
                                   OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CSSXR-SUB) = 'Y'
                                   MOVE WS-CSSXR-SUB
                                       TO WS-OLD-PAX-SUB
                                   PERFORM 5900-MOVE-CSSXR-FIELDS THRU  
                                           5900-MOVE-CSSXR-FIELDS-EXIT  
                               ELSE 
                                   MOVE WS-CSSXR-SUB
                                       TO WS-OLD-PAX-SUB
                                   PERFORM 6000-PREPARE-CSSXR-DEL THRU  
                                           6000-PREPARE-CSSXR-DEL-EXIT  
                               END-IF   
                           END-IF   
                       END-IF   
                   END-PERFORM  
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

       5800-LOAD-CSSXR-DETAILS-EXIT.
           EXIT.

           IF  RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) = 'Y'
               IF  CCP01-CSSXR-MOVE-CAR 
                   IF  CCP01-CAR-FOR-BKG-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) > 
                       SPACES   
                       MOVE CCP01-CAR-FOR-BKG-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE CCP01-CAR-FOR-SEQ-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE CACFOR-BOOK-NUM-FOR (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE CACFOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM-FOR (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB) 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                   END-IF   
                   IF  CCP01-CAR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'                      OR
                       WS-CAR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'   
                       MOVE WS-CAR-PAX-BOOK-NUM 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE WS-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE 0          TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE SPACES     TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                          RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME  
                                           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE ZEROES  TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE  
                                    OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)   
                                    RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME 
                                    OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)   
                   ELSE 
                       IF  CCP01-CSSXR-ACCEPT-AGENT-FLAG = 'Y'  OR  
                           WS-CSSXR-ACCEPT-AGENT-FLAG = 'Y' 
                           MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE 0      TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE 1      TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME TO
                                     RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME   
                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                           MOVE WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME TO
                                      RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME  
                                      OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) 
                           MOVE WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE   
                                         OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                           MOVE WS-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME   
                                         OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               ELSE 
                   MOVE CCP01-SSSR-SS-PAX-BKG-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                   MOVE CCP01-SSSR-SS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (CCP01-CSSXR-UP-SUB)  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                   IF  CCP01-SSSR-EXIST-IND = 'Y' OR
                       WS-SSSR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'  
                       MOVE WA-SSSR-PAX-BKG-NUM TO  
                                  RSP06-CSSXR-SSSR-PAX-BKG-NUM  
                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB 1)  
                       MOVE WA-SSSR-PAX-SEQ-NUM  TO 
                                    RSP06-CSSXR-SSSR-PAX-SEQ-NUM
                                 OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB 1)
                   END-IF   
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF  CCP01-CSSXR-MOVE-CAR 
                   IF  CCP01-CAR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'                      OR
                       WS-CAR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'   
                       MOVE WS-CAR-PAX-BOOK-NUM 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE WS-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM  
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE 0          TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE SPACES     TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME  
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                       RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME 
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                       MOVE ZEROES     TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                                       RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME  
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                   ELSE 
                       IF  CCP01-CSSXR-ACCEPT-AGENT-FLAG = 'Y'  
                           MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE 0      TO RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM   
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE 1      TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGENT-INFO-CNT
                                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
                           MOVE CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME TO 
                                     RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-FNAME   
                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                           MOVE CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME TO 
                                      RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-LNAME  
                                      OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) 
                           MOVE CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE 
                                      TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-DATE
                                      OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) 
                           MOVE CCP01-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME 
                                       TO RSP06-CSSXR-AGT-ACCEPT-TIME   
                                         OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)  
                       END-IF   
                   END-IF   
               ELSE 
                   IF  CCP01-SSSR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'     OR
                       WS-SSSR-EXIST-IND = 'Y'

                                   RSP06-CSSXR-SSSR-PAX-BKG-NUM

       6800-GET-ADDITIONAL-INFO.

       6810-ADD-INFO-TEXT.  
      D    MOVE '6810-ADD-INFO-TEXT'   TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

            MOVE 32561216              TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER.   

            FETCH NEXT ADD-INFO-DESC-REC WITHIN ADD-INFO-SET SET
               AT END GO TO 6800-GET-ADDITIONAL-INFO-EXIT   
                   ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

109247      MOVE 0                     TO PIPOL2-SUB.   

       6820-LOOP-THRU-DATA. 
      D    MOVE '6820-LOOP-THRU-DATA'  TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

           ADD 1                       TO WS-SUB.   

           IF  WS-SUB > 9   
               GO TO 6810-ADD-INFO-TEXT 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE AIDR-DATA (WS-SUB)     TO WS-CHAR-TABLE.

           IF  WS-CHAR-TABLE NOT > SPACES   
               GO TO 6820-LOOP-THRU-DATA
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-CHR (1) = '.' 
               MOVE SPACE              TO WS-CHR (1)
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-CHR (1) = ' ' OR  (( CCMV-TEXT-PTR + 64 ) > 2000 ) OR 
               PIPOL2-SUB = 0   
               ADD 1                   TO PIPOL2-SUB
               MOVE 1                  TO CCMV-MESSAGE-PTR  
               MOVE 35                 TO PIPOL2-POLICY-CODE
                                                          (PIPOL2-SUB)  
               MOVE SPACES             TO PIPOL2-POLICY-TEXT
                                                          (PIPOL2-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 65                     TO WS-LAST-CHAR. 


       6830-CHECK-FOR-LAST-CHAR.
      D    MOVE '6830-CHECK-FOR-LAST-CHAR'  
      D                                TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

           SUBTRACT 1                  FROM WS-LAST-CHAR.   

           IF  WS-LAST-CHAR = ZERO  
               GO TO 6820-LOOP-THRU-DATA
           END-IF.  

           IF  WS-CHR (WS-LAST-CHAR) > SPACES   
               GO TO 6840-ADD-ENTRY 
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 6830-CHECK-FOR-LAST-CHAR.  

       6840-ADD-ENTRY.  
      D    MOVE '6840-ADD-ENTRY'       TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

           SUBTRACT WS-LAST-CHAR       FROM 64 GIVING WS-LAST-CHAR. 
           STRING WS-CHAR-TABLE DELIMITED BY SIZE   
               INTO PIPOL2-POLICY-TEXT (PIPOL2-SUB) 
                   POINTER CCMV-TEXT-PTR.   
           SUBTRACT WS-LAST-CHAR       FROM CCMV-TEXT-PTR   
                                            GIVING CCMV-TEXT-PTR.   

           GO TO 6820-LOOP-THRU-DATA.

           EXIT.

                               7000-LOAD-FUNPASS-INDS-EXIT  
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM 6500-CALL-RSO25UGC THRU  
                   6500-CALL-RSO25UGC-EXIT. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-PAX-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-OLD-PAX-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
               MOVE 'N'                TO WS-PAX-FOUND-FLG

C35907  
CCLCOM*7300-CALL-SXC32EXC.  
CCLCOM*    MOVE '7300-CALL-SXC32EXC'   TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*    DISPLAY 'AT 7300-CALL-SXC32EXC ' UPON PRINTER.   
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*    MOVE LOW-VALUES             TO SXO32-I-BOOK-NUM. 
144449*    COPY DPP01INT.   
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*    IF  RSP06-IBR-SHORE-EXC-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD > 0 
CCLCOM*        MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
CCLCOM*                                TO SXO32-I-BOOK-NUM  
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*        CALL 'SXC32EXC' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
CCLCOM*                              DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
CCLCOM*                              SXO32-INPUT-DATA   
CCLCOM*                              SXO32-OUTPUT-DATA  
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*        IF  SXO32-O-ERROR-REASON = 'INVALID BOOKING NUMBER'  
CCLCOM*            GO TO 7200-OUTPUT-ERRMSG 
CCLCOM*        END-IF   
CCLCOM*    ELSE 
CCLCOM*        MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO SXO32-OUTPUT-DATA 
CCLCOM*    END-IF.  
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*7300-CALL-SXC32EXC-EXIT. 
CCLCOM*    EXIT.
CCLCOM*

      *THIS PARA TERMINATES THE PROGRAM NORMALLY.                      *
      ******************************************************************

FUNPAS 8000-POPULATE-RESP.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) > 0  
              GO TO 8000-POPULATE-RESP-EXIT.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW  TO ABGIR2-BOOK-NUM.

           IF RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW > SPACES
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                                   TO ABGIR2-GROUP-CONF-NUM 
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACES TO ABGIR2-GROUP-CONF-NUM  
           END-IF.  
FUNFIX     MOVE SPACES TO ABGIR2-SYNCH-ID.  
CQ9275     MOVE   RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE       OF RSP06-NEW   TO   
CQ9275            ABGIR2-SHIP-CODE-1
CQ9275     MOVE   RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE       OF RSP06-NEW   TO   
CQ9275            ABGIR2-SAIL-DATE-1
CQ9275     MOVE   RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS   OF RSP06-NEW   TO   
CQ9275            ABGIR2-SAIL-DUR-1.
CQ9275     MOVE RSP06-IBR-UPDATE-DATETIME OF RSP06-NEW  
CQ9275                               TO   ABGIR2-SYNCH-ID-1.

P90198     MOVE   RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-TABLE-ID OF RSP06-NEW   TO
P90198            ABGIR2-CHECKIN-TABLE-ID.  

P90198     PERFORM 8500-VALIDATE-STG-CHK THRU   
P90198             8500-VALIDATE-STG-CHK-EXIT.  
P90198     MOVE   WS-STAG-CHECK-IN-ENABLED  TO  
P90198            ABGIR2-CHECKIN-ENABLED-IND.   

           PERFORM 8005-PIAR-TO-ABGST2 THRU 8005-EXIT.  

CQ9427     MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-LOAD-CONTACT   
CQ9427                                WS-LOAD-ADDRESS

FUNPAS 8030-PICR-TO-PICRI2. 
      D    DISPLAY 'AT 8030-PICR-TO-PICRI2 ' PICRI2-SUB UPON PRINTER.   

           IF RSP06-PICR-RELATIONSHIP OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)
                    = 'SELF'
              GO TO 8030-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO PICRI2-SUB. 
           ADD 1 TO PICRI2-OCCURS.  

           MOVE SPACES            TO PICRI2-SEGMENT  (PICRI2-SUB)   
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)
                                  TO PICRI2-PAX-SEQ-NUM  (PICRI2-SUB).  
           MOVE RSP06-PICR-FIRST-NAME OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)
                                  TO PICRI2-FIRST-NAME (PICRI2-SUB).
           MOVE RSP06-PICR-LAST-NAME OF RSP06-NEW  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)
                                  TO PICRI2-LAST-NAME (PICRI2-SUB). 
           MOVE RSP06-PICR-RELATIONSHIP OF RSP06-NEW
                                                   (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB)
                                  TO PICRI2-RELATION (PICRI2-SUB).  

           PERFORM 8035-PICR-TO-APTEL2 THRU 8035-EXIT   
              VARYING PICRID-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                UNTIL PICRID-SUB > RSP06-PICR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW
                     (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB). 
FUNPAS 8030-EXIT.   
            EXIT.

                  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB PICRID-SUB) = SPACES 
              GO TO 8035-EXIT.

                  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB PICRID-SUB) > SPACE

                  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICR-SUB PICRID-SUB)  
                          TO APTEL2-PHONE-TYPE (APTEL2-SUB)

              MOVE 'H'    TO APTEL2-PHONE-TYPE (APTEL2-SUB).

            EXIT.

                                  (WS-DK-4 WS-PICH-SUB)

       8060-PICF-TO-ABGFD2. 
           MOVE SPACES TO WS-FLT-DIRECTION. 
           PERFORM VARYING PSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
              UNTIL PSUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)
            IF RSP06-PICF-FROM-CITY OF RSP06-NEW
                           (WS-DK-4 , PSUB) >  SPACES   
              IF RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 , PSUB)   
                    <= RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                 MOVE "A"  TO WS-FLT-DIRECTION  
              END-IF
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF WS-FLT-DIRECTION = 'A'
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 8060-CONTINUE-1 
           END-IF.  
           ADD 1        TO ABGFD2-OCCURS
           MOVE "A"  TO ABGFD2-DIRECTION-CODE (ABGFD2-OCCURS)   
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) TO 
                ABGFD2-PAX-SEQ-NUM  (ABGFD2-OCCURS).
       8060-CONTINUE-1. 
           MOVE SPACES TO WS-FLT-DIRECTION. 
           PERFORM VARYING PSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
             UNTIL PSUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
             IF RSP06-PICF-FROM-CITY OF RSP06-NEW   
                          (WS-DK-4 , PSUB) >  SPACES
              IF RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 , PSUB)   
                    >  RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                MOVE "T"  TO WS-FLT-DIRECTION   
              END-IF
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-FLT-DIRECTION = 'T'
              ADD 1 TO ABGFD2-OCCURS
              MOVE "T"  TO ABGFD2-DIRECTION-CODE (ABGFD2-OCCURS)
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) TO  
                      ABGFD2-PAX-SEQ-NUM  (ABGFD2-OCCURS)   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING PSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
              UNTIL PSUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)
              IF RSP06-PICF-FROM-CITY OF RSP06-NEW  
                           (WS-DK-4 , PSUB) >  SPACES   
                 PERFORM 8065-PICF-TO-ABFLT2 THRU 8065-EXIT 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

       8060-EXIT.   
            EXIT.

       8070-PTFR-TO-ABSPL2.

146816         GO TO 8070-EXIT  
146816     END-IF.

                   (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB) > SPACE
              CONTINUE

           MOVE SPACES       TO ABSPL2-SEGMENT           (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-CODE      (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE ZERO         TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-DATE      (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-YEARS     (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE SPACES       TO WS-PTFR-SERVICE-CODE.   

           MOVE 'XF'         TO WS-PTFR-SERVICE-TYPE.   
           IF RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                 (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'I'
              IF RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC OF RSP06-NEW   
                 (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'S' AND
144449           (SSP03-O-INTERNAL-CHANNEL-FLG = 1 AND  
144449            SSP03-O-CONSUMER-FLG = 0) 
                 MOVE 'TF'   TO WS-PTFR-SERVICE-TYPE
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD 
                             TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-DATE      (ABSPL2-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
                 (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'O'
              IF RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC OF RSP06-NEW   
                 (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'S'
                 MOVE 'TF'   TO WS-PTFR-SERVICE-TYPE
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD 
                             TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD 
                             TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS   
              PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
              MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
                             TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-DATE      (ABSPL2-SUB)   
           END-IF.  

           IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) = 'C'  
              MOVE RSP06-PTFR-LOC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW 
                     (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB)  
                             TO WS-PTFR-CITY-LOC
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'FFF'     TO WS-PTFR-CITY-LOC
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND OF RSP06-NEW
                     (WS-DK-4 WS-PTFR-SUB)  
                             TO WS-PTFR-DIRECTION.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)
                             TO ABSPL2-PAX-SEQ-NUM       (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 'OTH'        TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE WS-PTFR-SERVICE-CODE
                             TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-CODE      (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 0            TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-YEARS     (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 1            TO ABSPL2-QUANTITY          (ABSPL2-SUB).  

       8070-EXIT.   
            EXIT.   

FUNPAS 8080-PREG-TO-ABSPL2. 
      D    DISPLAY 'AT 8080-PREG-TO-ABSPL2 ' UPON PRINTER.  

           MOVE ZERO                   TO WS-TALLY. 
           MOVE SPACE                  TO WS-PAX-PREG-CODE. 
           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)   
              TALLYING WS-TALLY FOR ALL WS-PREG-CODE-Q. 
           IF WS-TALLY > ZERO   
              MOVE WS-PREG-CODE-Q      TO WS-PAX-PREG-CODE  
              GO TO 8080-PREG-TO-ABSPL2-CONT.   
           INSPECT RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)   
              TALLYING WS-TALLY FOR ALL WS-PREG-CODE-X. 
           IF WS-TALLY > ZERO   
              MOVE WS-PREG-CODE-X   TO WS-PAX-PREG-CODE.
           IF WS-PAX-PREG-CODE = SPACE  
              GO TO 8080-EXIT.  
       8080-PREG-TO-ABSPL2-CONT.
           ADD 1 TO ABSPL2-SUB. 
           IF ABSPL2-SUB > ABSPL2-SEGMENT-MAX   
              MOVE 22  TO CCMV-ERROR-SUB
              GO TO 8080-EXIT.  
           MOVE ABSPL2-SUB TO ABSPL2-OCCURS.
      D    DISPLAY ' 8080-PREG-TO-ABSPL2 ' ABSPL2-SUB  ' : '
      D              ABSPL2-OCCURS UPON PRINTER.

           MOVE SPACES       TO ABSPL2-SEGMENT           (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-CODE      (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE ZERO         TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-DATE      (ABSPL2-SUB)   
                                ABSPL2-SERVICE-YEARS     (ABSPL2-SUB).  

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)
                             TO ABSPL2-PAX-SEQ-NUM       (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 'MED'        TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-TYPE-CODE (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE WS-PAX-PREG-CODE
                             TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-CODE      (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 0            TO ABSPL2-SERVICE-YEARS     (ABSPL2-SUB).  
           MOVE 1            TO ABSPL2-QUANTITY          (ABSPL2-SUB).  

       8080-EXIT.   
            EXIT.   

P47261 8085-PICC-TO-YOUTHC. 
P47261D     DISPLAY ' 8085-PICC-TO-YOUTHC ' ' PICC EXIST : '
P47261D     RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND
P47261D                    OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4).  
P47261      IF (RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND
P47261                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)  = 'Y' )  
P47261             MOVE 'N' TO WS-DONE-ABACT2   
P47261                         WS-DONE-ABPIN2   
P47261                         WS-FOOD-ALG-DONE 
P47261                         WS-SIGN-OUT-DONE 
P47261                         WS-SWIM-ALD-DONE 
P47261                         WS-UPDATE-PICC   
P47261             PERFORM 8085-PICC-TO-ABONB2 THRU 8085-ONB2-EXIT  
P47261             PERFORM 8085-PICC-TO-ABACT2 THRU 8085-ABACT2-EXIT
P47261             MOVE 0 TO T-SUB W-SUB
P47261             PERFORM 8085-PICC-TO-ABPIN2 THRU 8085-PIN2-EXIT  
P47261                VARYING ABPIN2-SUB FROM ABPIN2-SUB BY 1   
P47261                  UNTIL DONE-ABPIN2   
P47261        END-IF.   
P47261  
P47261 8085-PICC-TO-YOUTHC-EXIT.
P47261      EXIT.   

P47261 8085-PICC-TO-ABONB2. 
P47261     ADD 1 TO ABONB2-SUB. 
P47261     IF ABONB2-SUB > ABONB2-SEGMENT-MAX   
P47261        GO TO 8085-ONB2-EXIT. 
P47261

P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-CHILD-PSN (ABONB2-SUB).
P47261  
P47261      IF RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) > 0

P47261                             TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE

P47261         MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-PAX-DOB (ABONB2-SUB)   
P47261      ELSE
P47261         MOVE ZEROES TO ABONB2-PAX-DOB (ABONB2-SUB)   
P47261      END-IF. 
P47261     MOVE 'Y'        TO ABONB2-PARTICIPATE-IND (ABONB2-SUB).  
P47261     MOVE RSP06-PICC-TERMS-ACCPT  
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-TERMS-ACCEPTED (ABONB2-SUB).

P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-PICC-PROGRAM-TYPE (ABONB2-SUB).

P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-TERMS-ACCEPTED-BY (ABONB2-SUB).

P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
P47261                     TO ABONB2-GUARDIAN-PSN (ABONB2-SUB). 
209286     MOVE RSP06-PICC-TERMS-ACCPT-BY-BNUM  
209286               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) 
209286                   TO ABONB2-TERMS-ACCEPTED-BY-BKNUM (ABONB2-SUB).
P47261

P47261                  WS-SWIM-ALD-DONE = 'N'

P47261                OF  RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4)

P47261 8085-PICC-TO-ABPIN2.

P47261           OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) > SPACES AND

P47261        IF WS-DUPLICATE-FOUND = 'Y'

P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB)

P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) 
P47261                     TO ABPIN2-FIRST-NAME (ABPIN2-SUB).   
P47261     MOVE RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-LAST-NAME   
P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) 
P47261                     TO ABPIN2-LAST-NAME (ABPIN2-SUB).

P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) 
P47261                     TO ABPIN2-GUAR-PSN (ABPIN2-SUB).

P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) 
P47261                     TO ABPIN2-RELATION (ABPIN2-SUB).

P47261             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB)

P47261                               WSPIN2-CHILD-PSN (W-SUB)
