P47261                               WSPIN2-FIRST-NAME (W-SUB)  
P47261     MOVE ABPIN2-LAST-NAME (ABPIN2-SUB)  TO   
P47261                               WSPIN2-LAST-NAME (W-SUB)   
P47261     MOVE ABPIN2-GUAR-PSN  (ABPIN2-SUB)  TO   
P47261                               WSPIN2-GUAR-PSN  (W-SUB)   
P47261     MOVE ABPIN2-RELATION  (ABPIN2-SUB)  TO   
P47261                               WSPIN2-RELATION  (W-SUB)   
P47261     MOVE ABPIN2-GUAR-BKG-NUM (ABPIN2-SUB) TO 
P47261                               WSPIN2-GUAR-BKG-NUM (W-SUB).   
P47261  
P47261 8085-PIN2-EXIT.  
P47261     EXIT.

P47261 8086-ELIMINATE-DUPS. 
P47261     PERFORM VARYING X-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL X-SUB > W-SUB
P47261       IF RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4) =  
P47261                       WSPIN2-CHILD-PSN (X-SUB) AND   
P47261          RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-FIRST-NAME  
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) = 
P47261                       WSPIN2-FIRST-NAME (X-SUB) AND  
P47261          RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-LAST-NAME   
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) = 
P47261                        WSPIN2-LAST-NAME (X-SUB) AND  
P47261          RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-PSN 
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) = 
P47261                       ABPIN2-GUAR-PSN (X-SUB) AND
P47261          RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-RELATION
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) = 
P47261                        ABPIN2-RELATION (X-SUB) AND   
P47261          RSP06-PICC-GUARDIAN-BOOK-NUM
P47261               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-DK-4 T-SUB) = 
P47261                        WSPIN2-GUAR-BKG-NUM (X-SUB)   
P47261          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DUPLICATE-FOUND  
P47261        END-IF
P47261      END-PERFORM.
       8086-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

148695 8300-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB.

148695     MOVE 0 TO WS-NEW-SUB 
148695               WS-PAX-MOB-CNT.

148695 8310-NEW-NEXT-PAX.   

148695     ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-SUB  
148695              WS-PAX-MOB-CNT. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
148695        SUBTRACT 1              FROM WS-PAX-MOB-CNT   
148695        GO TO 8330-FIND-SAME-MOB-NUM  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     MOVE SPACES                TO WS-PAX-MOB-TBL (WS-NEW-SUB).   

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)= 'N'  
148695        GO TO 8310-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-NEW-PH-SUB >
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-PH-SUB >   
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8310-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     MOVE 0 TO WS-OLD-SUB.

148695 8320-OLD-NEXT-PAX.   

148695     ADD 1 TO WS-OLD-SUB. 

148695     IF WS-OLD-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD   
148695        GO TO 8310-NEW-NEXT-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)= 'N'  
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
148695           RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND   
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
148695           RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
148695        END-IF
148695        GO TO 8320-OLD-NEXT-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-OLD-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-OLD-PH-SUB >
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD   
148695                                  (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-OLD-PH-SUB >   
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)  
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
148695        END-IF
148695        GO TO 8320-OLD-NEXT-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                      (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
148695                      (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) (7:10) = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD 
148695                              (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) (7:10)   
148695           AND
148695           (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD
148695                              (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) (1:6) =  
148695                                             '000000' OR '000001')
148695           AND
148695           (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)(1:6) =   
148695                                             '000000' OR '000001')
148695           CONTINUE   
148695        ELSE  
148695           IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =
148695              RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND  
148695              RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) = 
148695              RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB)   
148695              IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 1   
148695                 MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD   
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)   
148695                      TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)   
148695              END-IF  
148695              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB)  
148695           END-IF 
148695           GO TO 8320-OLD-NEXT-PAX
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =  
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) AND
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB) =   
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OLD-SUB) 
148695        MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)   
148695            TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)   
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 1   
148695           AND RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)   
148695                                                 NOT = 'Y' AND 'I'
148695           MOVE 'Y'   
148695            TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)   
148695        END-IF
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-OLD 
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) OR
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD  
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) OR
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-OLD  
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB) OR
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                                 (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-OLD  
148695                                 (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)   
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
148695        ELSE  
148695           MOVE 'N' TO WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF   

148695     MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-OLD  
148695                      (WS-OLD-SUB WS-OLD-PH-SUB)  
148695            TO WS-OLD-PREF-IND (WS-NEW-SUB).  

148695     GO TO 8320-OLD-NEXT-PAX. 

148695 8330-FIND-SAME-MOB-NUM.  

148695     MOVE 0 TO WS-CUR-SUB.

148695 8340-NEXT-CURR-PAX.  

148695     ADD 1 TO WS-CUR-SUB. 

148695     IF WS-CUR-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
148695        GO TO 8300-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)= 'N'  
148695        GO TO 8340-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-CUR-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-CUR-PH-SUB >
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-CUR-PH-SUB >   
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-CUR-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8340-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     MOVE 0 TO WS-NEW-SUB.

148695 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX.   

148695     ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-SUB. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
148695        GO TO 8360-UPDATE-PREF-IND
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)= 'N'  
148695        GO TO 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF WS-NEW-SUB = WS-CUR-SUB   
148695        GO TO 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-NEW-PH-SUB >
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-PH-SUB >   
148695                   RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                      (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                      (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) (7:10) = 
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (7:10)   
148695           AND
148695           (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (1:6) =  
148695                                             '000000' OR '000001')
148695           AND
148695           (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)(1:6) =   
148695                                             '000000' OR '000001')
148695           CONTINUE   
148695        ELSE  
148695           GO TO 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND  
148695     ELSE 
148695        MOVE 'N' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
148695                                                NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
148695           OR (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695                                                NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DIFF-PREF-IND   
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  
148695     IF WS-DIFF-PREF-IND = 'Y'
148695        IF (WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'Y' AND  
148695            WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'Y') 
148695           ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
148695           MOVE 0150 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
148695           MOVE 'CCR21OBD'
148695                     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)
148695           PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
148695              THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
148695           GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
148695        END-IF
148695        IF (WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'N' AND  
148695            WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'Y') 
148695           MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 1
148695              MOVE 1  
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           END-IF 
148695           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 1
148695              OR RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 1
148695              IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695                                                 NOT = 'Y' AND 'I'
148695                 MOVE 'Y' 
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695              END-IF  
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695        IF (WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'Y' AND  
148695            WS-CHANGE-MOB-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'N') 
148695           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 1
148695              MOVE 1  
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           END-IF 
148695           IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 1
148695              MOVE 'Y'
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                    (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695           END-IF 
148695     END-IF.  


148695     GO TO 8350-NEXT-NEW-PAX. 

148695 8360-UPDATE-PREF-IND.
148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695                                                         <= SPACES
148695        MOVE SPACES   
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695     ELSE 
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 'N'
148695           MOVE 'N'   
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695        ELSE  
148695           IF WS-OLD-PREF-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'Y'  
148695              MOVE 'Y'
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695           ELSE   
148695              MOVE 'I'
148695              TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND OF RSP06-NEW   
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     GO TO 8340-NEXT-CURR-PAX.

148695 8300-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT.   
148695     EXIT.

148695 8400-DELETE-PHONE.   

148695     IF ABGST1-GUEST-ACTION-CODE (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'U'   
148695        GO TO 8400-DELETE-PHONE-EXIT  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF WS-PAX-PHONE-CNT >= RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 
148695                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8400-DELETE-PHONE-EXIT  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-PSUB FROM 1 BY 1  
148695             UNTIL WS-PSUB > RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
148695                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695        MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-DELETE-PHONE-IND
148695        PERFORM VARYING ABCTEL-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695                UNTIL ABCTEL-SUB > ABCTEL-OCCURS  
148695           IF (ABCTEL-TEL-PAX-SEQ-NUM (ABCTEL-SUB) =  
148695                  RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB))
148695              IF ABCTEL-PHONE-TYPE (ABCTEL-SUB) = 'H' OR 'C'  
148695                 CONTINUE 
148695              ELSE
148695                 MOVE 'H'       TO ABCTEL-PHONE-TYPE (ABCTEL-SUB) 
148695              END-IF  
148695              IF ABCTEL-PHONE-TYPE (ABCTEL-SUB) = 
148695                            RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE
148695                             OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB)
148695                 MOVE 'N'       TO WS-DELETE-PHONE-IND
148695              END-IF  
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-PERFORM   
148695        IF WS-DELETE-PHONE-IND = 'Y' AND  
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE 
148695                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB) = 'H'
148695           ADD 1     TO CCMV-ADV-SUB  
148695           MOVE 0151 TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-CODE (CCMV-ADV-SUB) 
148695           MOVE 'CCR21OBD'
148695                     TO MSGHDR-ADVISE-SOURCE(CCMV-ADV-SUB)
148695           PERFORM CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG  
148695              THRU CCP75-OUTPUT-CCERRMSG-EXIT 
148695           GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
148695        END-IF
148695        IF WS-DELETE-PHONE-IND = 'Y'  
148695           IF WS-PSUB =   
148695               RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695             SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
148695                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695           ELSE   
148695             MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT   
148695                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695                    TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT 
148695             MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW  
148695                          (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT) 
148695                    TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL OF RSP06-NEW 
148695                          (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB WS-PSUB)
148695             SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT
148695                                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-OLD-PAX-SUB)
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695 8400-DELETE-PHONE-EXIT.  
148695     EXIT.

P90198 8500-CHECK-FLT-ARR-TIME. 
           MOVE 0 TO WS-FLT-ARRIVAL-TIME
           PERFORM VARYING PAX-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
                           PAX-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
              PERFORM VARYING WS-DATE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                      UNTIL WS-DATE-SUB >   
                      RSP06-PICF-CNT  OF RSP06-NEW  (PAX-SUB)   
                 IF RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                                  (PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB) =   
                                   RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW 
                    MOVE RSP06-PICF-ARRIVE-TIME OF RSP06-NEW
                 (PAX-SUB WS-DATE-SUB) TO WS-FLT-ARRIVAL-TIME   
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
           END-PERFORM. 
           IF WS-TRANSFER-DURATION > 0  
              COMPUTE WS-FLT-ARRIVAL-TIME = 
                   WS-FLT-ARRIVAL-TIME + WS-TRANSFER-DURATION.

      *THIS PARA IS TO MONITOR THE FLOW OF THE PROGRAM WHEN THE        *
      *TEST    MODE   IS ON.                                           *
      ******************************************************************

      D9200-DB-PARA.
      D    MOVE '9200-DB-PARA'         TO WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME.  

      D    PERFORM 9300-ADD-PARA.   

      D    ADD 1                       TO WS-DB-LBL-SUB.
      D 
      D    IF  WS-DB-LBL-SUB > 9600                                   OR
      D        WS-DB-LBL-SUB > WS-DB-LBL-COUNTER
      D        GO TO 9200-DB-PARA-EXIT  
      D    END-IF.  
      D 
      D    MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-DB-PARA-HOLD.  
      D    MOVE WS-DB-LBL-PARA (WS-DB-LBL-SUB)  
      D                                TO WS-DB-PARA-1. 
      D    ADD 1                       TO WS-DB-LBL-SUB.
      D    MOVE WS-DB-LBL-PARA (WS-DB-LBL-SUB)  
      D                                TO WS-DB-PARA-2. 
      D    ADD 1                       TO WS-DB-LBL-SUB.
      D    MOVE WS-DB-LBL-PARA (WS-DB-LBL-SUB)  
      D                                TO WS-DB-PARA-3. 
      D    DISPLAY WS-DB-PARA-HOLD UPON PRINTER.
      D 
      D    GO TO 9200-DB-PARA.  
      D 
      D9200-DB-PARA-EXIT.   
      D    EXIT.

      ******************************************************************
      *THIS PARA ADDS THE PARA NAME TO THE TABLE.                      *
      ******************************************************************

      D9300-ADD-PARA.   
      D 
      D    IF  WS-DB-LBL-SUB NOT > 9600 
      D        ADD 1                   TO WS-DB-LBL-SUB 
      D        MOVE WS-DB-LBL-PARA-NAME 
      D                                TO   
      D                                  WS-DB-LBL-PARA (WS-DB-LBL-SUB).
      D9300-ADD-PARA-EXIT.  
      D    EXIT.
